#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            C[i * N + j] = sum;
        }
    }
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            N = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<double> A(N * N);
    std::vector<double> B(N * N);
    std::vector<double> C(N * N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    matrixMultiply(A, B, C, N);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

 // Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
-constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
+__host__ __device__ constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
     return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
 }
 
-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Both inputs use the same deterministic initialization as the CPU benchmark.
+__global__ void initMatrix(double* A, double* B, const size_t N) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < N * N; index += stride) {
+        const double value = getPseudoRndValue(N, index / N, index % N);
+        A[index] = value;
+        B[index] = value;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr unsigned tileRows = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileCols = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileDepth = 16;
+constexpr unsigned threadsX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned threadsY = 8;
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive columns, and each thread keeps an 8x2 output
+// tile in registers. Shared input tiles are reused for 4096 output elements.
+template <bool fullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[tileRows][tileDepth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[tileDepth][tileCols];
+
+    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned thread = ty * threadsX + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tileRows;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileCols;
+    double sums[tileRows / threadsY][tileCols / threadsX] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += tileDepth) {
+        // Coalesced global loads; edge tiles are zero padded.
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned index = thread; index < tileRows * tileDepth;
+             index += threadsX * threadsY) {
+            const unsigned row = index / tileDepth;
+            const unsigned k = index % tileDepth;
+            aTile[row][k] = (fullTiles || (rowBase + row < N && base + k < N))
+                ? A[(rowBase + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned index = thread; index < tileDepth * tileCols;
+             index += threadsX * threadsY) {
+            const unsigned k = index / tileCols;
+            const unsigned col = index % tileCols;
+            bTile[k][col] = (fullTiles || (base + k < N && colBase + col < N))
+                ? B[(base + k) * N + colBase + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        // Accumulate in increasing k order, using full-precision double FMA.
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < tileDepth; ++k) {
+            double aValues[tileRows / threadsY];
+            double bValues[tileCols / threadsX];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+                aValues[row] = aTile[ty + row * threadsY][k];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+                bValues[col] = bTile[k][tx + col * threadsX];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+                    sums[row][col] = fma(aValues[row], bValues[col], sums[row][col]);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // All readers must finish before the next tile overwrites shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+        const size_t i = rowBase + ty + row * threadsY;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+            const size_t j = colBase + tx + col * threadsX;
+            if (fullTiles || (i < N && j < N)) {
+                C[i * N + j] = sums[row][col];
+            }
         }
     }
 }
 
-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+// Allocate once, outside the timed multiplication, and release on every exit.
+class DeviceMatrices {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceMatrices(size_t N) : elements_(N * N) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(0), "Selecting CUDA device");
+        if (elements_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                 3 * elements_ * sizeof(double)), "Allocating GPU matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceMatrices() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
         }
     }
+
+    DeviceMatrices(const DeviceMatrices&) = delete;
+    DeviceMatrices& operator=(const DeviceMatrices&) = delete;
+
+    double* A() const { return data_; }
+    double* B() const { return data_ == nullptr ? nullptr : data_ + elements_; }
+    double* C() const { return data_ == nullptr ? nullptr : data_ + 2 * elements_; }
+
+private:
+    size_t elements_;
+    double* data_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+void matrixMultiply(const DeviceMatrices& matrices, const size_t N) {
+    if (N == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const dim3 threads(threadsX, threadsY);
+    const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N + tileCols - 1) / tileCols),
+                      static_cast<unsigned>((N + tileRows - 1) / tileRows));
+    if (N % tileRows == 0 && N % tileCols == 0 && N % tileDepth == 0) {
+        matrixMultiplyKernel<true><<<blocks, threads>>>(matrices.A(), matrices.B(), matrices.C(), N);
+    } else {
+        matrixMultiplyKernel<false><<<blocks, threads>>>(matrices.A(), matrices.B(), matrices.C(), N);
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching CUDA multiplication");
+    checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "Completing CUDA multiplication");
 }

+__host__ __device__ constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
     return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
 }
 
-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Both inputs use the same deterministic initialization as the CPU benchmark.
+__global__ void initMatrix(double* A, double* B, const size_t N) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < N * N; index += stride) {
+        const double value = getPseudoRndValue(N, index / N, index % N);
+        A[index] = value;
+        B[index] = value;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr unsigned tileRows = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileCols = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileDepth = 16;
+constexpr unsigned threadsX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned threadsY = 8;
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive columns, and each thread keeps an 8x2 output
+// tile in registers. Shared input tiles are reused for 4096 output elements.
+template <bool fullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[tileRows][tileDepth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[tileDepth][tileCols];
+
+    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned thread = ty * threadsX + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tileRows;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileCols;
+    double sums[tileRows / threadsY][tileCols / threadsX] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += tileDepth) {
+        // Coalesced global loads; edge tiles are zero padded.
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned index = thread; index < tileRows * tileDepth;
+             index += threadsX * threadsY) {
+            const unsigned row = index / tileDepth;
+            const unsigned k = index % tileDepth;
+            aTile[row][k] = (fullTiles || (rowBase + row < N && base + k < N))
+                ? A[(rowBase + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned index = thread; index < tileDepth * tileCols;
+             index += threadsX * threadsY) {
+            const unsigned k = index / tileCols;
+            const unsigned col = index % tileCols;
+            bTile[k][col] = (fullTiles || (base + k < N && colBase + col < N))
+                ? B[(base + k) * N + colBase + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        // Accumulate in increasing k order, using full-precision double FMA.
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < tileDepth; ++k) {
+            double aValues[tileRows / threadsY];
+            double bValues[tileCols / threadsX];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+                aValues[row] = aTile[ty + row * threadsY][k];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+                bValues[col] = bTile[k][tx + col * threadsX];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+                    sums[row][col] = fma(aValues[row], bValues[col], sums[row][col]);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // All readers must finish before the next tile overwrites shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+        const size_t i = rowBase + ty + row * threadsY;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+            const size_t j = colBase + tx + col * threadsX;
+            if (fullTiles || (i < N && j < N)) {
+                C[i * N + j] = sums[row][col];
+            }
         }
     }
 }

+// Allocate once, outside the timed multiplication, and release on every exit.
+class DeviceMatrices {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceMatrices(size_t N) : elements_(N * N) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(0), "Selecting CUDA device");
+        if (elements_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                 3 * elements_ * sizeof(double)), "Allocating GPU matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceMatrices() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
         }
     }
+
+    DeviceMatrices(const DeviceMatrices&) = delete;
+    DeviceMatrices& operator=(const DeviceMatrices&) = delete;
+
+    double* A() const { return data_; }
+    double* B() const { return data_ == nullptr ? nullptr : data_ + elements_; }
+    double* C() const { return data_ == nullptr ? nullptr : data_ + 2 * elements_; }
+
+private:
+    size_t elements_;
+    double* data_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+void matrixMultiply(const DeviceMatrices& matrices, const size_t N) {
+    if (N == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const dim3 threads(threadsX, threadsY);
+    const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N + tileCols - 1) / tileCols),
+                      static_cast<unsigned>((N + tileRows - 1) / tileRows));
+    if (N % tileRows == 0 && N % tileCols == 0 && N % tileDepth == 0) {
+        matrixMultiplyKernel<true><<<blocks, threads>>>(matrices.A(), matrices.B(), matrices.C(), N);
+    } else {
+        matrixMultiplyKernel<false><<<blocks, threads>>>(matrices.A(), matrices.B(), matrices.C(), N);
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching CUDA multiplication");
+    checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "Completing CUDA multiplication");
 }

+constexpr unsigned tileRows = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileCols = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileDepth = 16;
+constexpr unsigned threadsX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned threadsY = 8;

+// Each warp accesses consecutive columns, and each thread keeps an 8x2 output
+// tile in registers. Shared input tiles are reused for 4096 output elements.
+template <bool fullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[tileRows][tileDepth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[tileDepth][tileCols];
+
+    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned thread = ty * threadsX + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tileRows;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileCols;
+    double sums[tileRows / threadsY][tileCols / threadsX] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += tileDepth) {
+        // Coalesced global loads; edge tiles are zero padded.
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned index = thread; index < tileRows * tileDepth;
+             index += threadsX * threadsY) {
+            const unsigned row = index / tileDepth;
+            const unsigned k = index % tileDepth;
+            aTile[row][k] = (fullTiles || (rowBase + row < N && base + k < N))
+                ? A[(rowBase + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned index = thread; index < tileDepth * tileCols;
+             index += threadsX * threadsY) {
+            const unsigned k = index / tileCols;
+            const unsigned col = index % tileCols;
+            bTile[k][col] = (fullTiles || (base + k < N && colBase + col < N))
+                ? B[(base + k) * N + colBase + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        // Accumulate in increasing k order, using full-precision double FMA.
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < tileDepth; ++k) {
+            double aValues[tileRows / threadsY];
+            double bValues[tileCols / threadsX];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+                aValues[row] = aTile[ty + row * threadsY][k];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+                bValues[col] = bTile[k][tx + col * threadsX];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+                    sums[row][col] = fma(aValues[row], bValues[col], sums[row][col]);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // All readers must finish before the next tile overwrites shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+        const size_t i = rowBase + ty + row * threadsY;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+            const size_t j = colBase + tx + col * threadsX;
+            if (fullTiles || (i < N && j < N)) {
+                C[i * N + j] = sums[row][col];
+            }

#include <cuda_runtime.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cerrno>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
__host__ __device__ constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
    }
}

// Both inputs use the same deterministic initialization as the CPU benchmark.
__global__ void initMatrix(double* A, double* B, const size_t N) {
    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         index < N * N; index += stride) {
        const double value = getPseudoRndValue(N, index / N, index % N);
        A[index] = value;
        B[index] = value;
    }
}

constexpr unsigned tileRows = 64;
constexpr unsigned tileCols = 64;
constexpr unsigned tileDepth = 16;
constexpr unsigned threadsX = 32;
constexpr unsigned threadsY = 8;

// Each warp accesses consecutive columns, and each thread keeps an 8x2 output
// tile in registers. Shared input tiles are reused for 4096 output elements.
template <bool fullTiles>
__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
                                     double* __restrict__ C, const size_t N) {
    __shared__ double aTile[tileRows][tileDepth + 1];
    __shared__ double bTile[tileDepth][tileCols];

    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x;
    const unsigned ty = threadIdx.y;
    const unsigned thread = ty * threadsX + tx;
    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tileRows;
    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileCols;
    double sums[tileRows / threadsY][tileCols / threadsX] = {};

    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += tileDepth) {
        // Coalesced global loads; edge tiles are zero padded.
        #pragma unroll
        for (unsigned index = thread; index < tileRows * tileDepth;
             index += threadsX * threadsY) {
            const unsigned row = index / tileDepth;
            const unsigned k = index % tileDepth;
            aTile[row][k] = (fullTiles || (rowBase + row < N && base + k < N))
                ? A[(rowBase + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
        }
        #pragma unroll
        for (unsigned index = thread; index < tileDepth * tileCols;
             index += threadsX * threadsY) {
            const unsigned k = index / tileCols;
            const unsigned col = index % tileCols;
            bTile[k][col] = (fullTiles || (base + k < N && colBase + col < N))
                ? B[(base + k) * N + colBase + col] : 0.0;
        }
        __syncthreads();

        // Accumulate in increasing k order, using full-precision double FMA.
        #pragma unroll
        for (unsigned k = 0; k < tileDepth; ++k) {
            double aValues[tileRows / threadsY];
            double bValues[tileCols / threadsX];
            #pragma unroll
            for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
                aValues[row] = aTile[ty + row * threadsY][k];
            }
            #pragma unroll
            for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
                bValues[col] = bTile[k][tx + col * threadsX];
            }
            #pragma unroll
            for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
                #pragma unroll
                for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
                    sums[row][col] = fma(aValues[row], bValues[col], sums[row][col]);
                }
            }
        }
        // All readers must finish before the next tile overwrites shared memory.
        __syncthreads();
    }

    #pragma unroll
    for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
        const size_t i = rowBase + ty + row * threadsY;
        #pragma unroll
        for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
            const size_t j = colBase + tx + col * threadsX;
            if (fullTiles || (i < N && j < N)) {
                C[i * N + j] = sums[row][col];
            }
        }
    }
}

// Allocate once, outside the timed multiplication, and release on every exit.
class DeviceMatrices {
public:
    explicit DeviceMatrices(size_t N) : elements_(N * N) {
        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(0), "Selecting CUDA device");
        if (elements_ != 0) {
            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
                                 3 * elements_ * sizeof(double)), "Allocating GPU matrices");
        }
    }

    ~DeviceMatrices() {
        if (data_ != nullptr) {
            cudaFree(data_);
        }
    }

    DeviceMatrices(const DeviceMatrices&) = delete;
    DeviceMatrices& operator=(const DeviceMatrices&) = delete;

    double* A() const { return data_; }
    double* B() const { return data_ == nullptr ? nullptr : data_ + elements_; }
    double* C() const { return data_ == nullptr ? nullptr : data_ + 2 * elements_; }

private:
    size_t elements_;
    double* data_ = nullptr;
};

void matrixMultiply(const DeviceMatrices& matrices, const size_t N) {
    if (N == 0) {
        return;
    }
    const dim3 threads(threadsX, threadsY);
    const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N + tileCols - 1) / tileCols),
                      static_cast<unsigned>((N + tileRows - 1) / tileRows));
    if (N % tileRows == 0 && N % tileCols == 0 && N % tileDepth == 0) {
        matrixMultiplyKernel<true><<<blocks, threads>>>(matrices.A(), matrices.B(), matrices.C(), N);
    } else {
        matrixMultiplyKernel<false><<<blocks, threads>>>(matrices.A(), matrices.B(), matrices.C(), N);
    }
    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching CUDA multiplication");
    checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "Completing CUDA multiplication");
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    if (N == 0) {
        return true;
    }
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += getPseudoRndValue(N, i, k) * getPseudoRndValue(N, k, j);
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (!std::isfinite(actual) || relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            const char* value = argv[++i];
            char* end = nullptr;
            errno = 0;
            const unsigned long long parsed = strtoull(value, &end, 10);
            if (errno != 0 || end == value || *end != '\0' || *value == '-' ||
                parsed > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
                fprintf(stderr, "Invalid matrix size: %s\n", value);
                return 1;
            }
            N = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Check the combined allocation before forming N*N or multiplying bytes.
    if (N != 0 && N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double) / 3 / N) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Matrix size is too large\n");
        return 1;
    }

    try {
    DeviceMatrices matrices(N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    if (N != 0) {
        const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>((N * N + 255) / 256, 4096));
        initMatrix<<<blocks, 256>>>(matrices.A(), matrices.B(), N);
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching CUDA initialization");
        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "Completing CUDA initialization");
    }
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    
    matrixMultiply(matrices, N);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", static_cast<long long>(duration.count()));
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    const double seconds = std::chrono::duration<double>(end - start).count();
    double gflops = seconds > 0.0 ? (2.0 * N * N * N) / seconds / 1e9 : 0.0;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);

    // Transfer results only when the existing output/validation options need them.
    std::vector<double> C;
    if (printResults || validate) {
        C.resize(N * N);
        if (!C.empty()) {
            checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(C.data(), matrices.C(), C.size() * sizeof(double),
                                 cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "Copying CUDA results");
        }
    }
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Matrix multiplication failed: %s\n", error.what());
        return 1;
    }
}

# Prefer the active CUDA toolkit, while respecting an explicitly chosen compiler.
if(NOT CMAKE_CUDA_COMPILER AND NOT DEFINED ENV{CUDACXX})
    set(MATMUL_CUDA_HINTS /usr/local/cuda/bin)
    if(DEFINED ENV{CUDAToolkit_ROOT})
        list(PREPEND MATMUL_CUDA_HINTS "$ENV{CUDAToolkit_ROOT}/bin")
    endif()
    if(CUDAToolkit_ROOT)
        list(PREPEND MATMUL_CUDA_HINTS "${CUDAToolkit_ROOT}/bin")
    endif()
    find_program(MATMUL_NVCC nvcc HINTS ${MATMUL_CUDA_HINTS})
    if(MATMUL_NVCC)
        set(CMAKE_CUDA_COMPILER "${MATMUL_NVCC}" CACHE FILEPATH "CUDA compiler")
    endif()
endif()

    set(CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native)
endif()

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(matmul m)

 // Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
-constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
+__host__ __device__ constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
     return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
 }
 
-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Both inputs use the same deterministic initialization as the CPU benchmark.
+__global__ void initMatrix(double* A, double* B, const size_t N) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < N * N; index += stride) {
+        const double value = getPseudoRndValue(N, index / N, index % N);
+        A[index] = value;
+        B[index] = value;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr unsigned tileRows = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileCols = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileDepth = 16;
+constexpr unsigned threadsX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned threadsY = 8;
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive columns, and each thread keeps an 8x2 output
+// tile in registers. Shared input tiles are reused for 4096 output elements.
+template <bool fullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[tileRows][tileDepth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[tileDepth][tileCols];
+
+    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned thread = ty * threadsX + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tileRows;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileCols;
+    double sums[tileRows / threadsY][tileCols / threadsX] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += tileDepth) {
+        // Coalesced global loads; edge tiles are zero padded.
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned index = thread; index < tileRows * tileDepth;
+             index += threadsX * threadsY) {
+            const unsigned row = index / tileDepth;
+            const unsigned k = index % tileDepth;
+            aTile[row][k] = (fullTiles || (rowBase + row < N && base + k < N))
+                ? A[(rowBase + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned index = thread; index < tileDepth * tileCols;
+             index += threadsX * threadsY) {
+            const unsigned k = index / tileCols;
+            const unsigned col = index % tileCols;
+            bTile[k][col] = (fullTiles || (base + k < N && colBase + col < N))
+                ? B[(base + k) * N + colBase + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        // Accumulate in increasing k order, using full-precision double FMA.
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < tileDepth; ++k) {
+            double aValues[tileRows / threadsY];
+            double bValues[tileCols / threadsX];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+                aValues[row] = aTile[ty + row * threadsY][k];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+                bValues[col] = bTile[k][tx + col * threadsX];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+                    sums[row][col] = fma(aValues[row], bValues[col], sums[row][col]);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // All readers must finish before the next tile overwrites shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+        const size_t i = rowBase + ty + row * threadsY;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+            const size_t j = colBase + tx + col * threadsX;
+            if (fullTiles || (i < N && j < N)) {
+                C[i * N + j] = sums[row][col];
+            }
         }
     }
 }
 
-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+// Allocate once, outside the timed multiplication, and release on every exit.
+class DeviceMatrices {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceMatrices(size_t N) : elements_(N * N) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(0), "Selecting CUDA device");
+        if (elements_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                 3 * elements_ * sizeof(double)), "Allocating GPU matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceMatrices() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
         }
     }
+
+    DeviceMatrices(const DeviceMatrices&) = delete;
+    DeviceMatrices& operator=(const DeviceMatrices&) = delete;
+
+    double* A() const { return data_; }
+    double* B() const { return data_ == nullptr ? nullptr : data_ + elements_; }
+    double* C() const { return data_ == nullptr ? nullptr : data_ + 2 * elements_; }
+
+private:
+    size_t elements_;
+    double* data_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+void matrixMultiply(const DeviceMatrices& matrices, const size_t N) {
+    if (N == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const dim3 threads(threadsX, threadsY);
+    const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N + tileCols - 1) / tileCols),
+                      static_cast<unsigned>((N + tileRows - 1) / tileRows));
+    if (N % tileRows == 0 && N % tileCols == 0 && N % tileDepth == 0) {
+        matrixMultiplyKernel<true><<<blocks, threads>>>(matrices.A(), matrices.B(), matrices.C(), N);
+    } else {
+        matrixMultiplyKernel<false><<<blocks, threads>>>(matrices.A(), matrices.B(), matrices.C(), N);
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching CUDA multiplication");
+    checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "Completing CUDA multiplication");
 }

The CUDA implementation is in place. It keeps inputs and outputs on the GPU, copies results back when validation or output needs them, and handles partial tiles for arbitrary matrix sizes. The build is progressing with the installed CUDA 12.6 toolkit; next I’ll compare results and check GPU memory access.

+__host__ __device__ constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
     return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Both inputs use the same deterministic initialization as the CPU benchmark.
+__global__ void initMatrix(double* A, double* B, const size_t N) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < N * N; index += stride) {
+        const double value = getPseudoRndValue(N, index / N, index % N);
+        A[index] = value;
+        B[index] = value;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr unsigned tileRows = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileCols = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileDepth = 16;
+constexpr unsigned threadsX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned threadsY = 8;
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive columns, and each thread keeps an 8x2 output
+// tile in registers. Shared input tiles are reused for 4096 output elements.
+template <bool fullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[tileRows][tileDepth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[tileDepth][tileCols];
+
+    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned thread = ty * threadsX + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tileRows;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileCols;
+    double sums[tileRows / threadsY][tileCols / threadsX] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += tileDepth) {
+        // Coalesced global loads; edge tiles are zero padded.
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned index = thread; index < tileRows * tileDepth;
+             index += threadsX * threadsY) {
+            const unsigned row = index / tileDepth;
+            const unsigned k = index % tileDepth;
+            aTile[row][k] = (fullTiles || (rowBase + row < N && base + k < N))
+                ? A[(rowBase + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned index = thread; index < tileDepth * tileCols;
+             index += threadsX * threadsY) {
+            const unsigned k = index / tileCols;
+            const unsigned col = index % tileCols;
+            bTile[k][col] = (fullTiles || (base + k < N && colBase + col < N))
+                ? B[(base + k) * N + colBase + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        // Accumulate in increasing k order, using full-precision double FMA.
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < tileDepth; ++k) {
+            double aValues[tileRows / threadsY];
+            double bValues[tileCols / threadsX];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+                aValues[row] = aTile[ty + row * threadsY][k];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+                bValues[col] = bTile[k][tx + col * threadsX];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+                    sums[row][col] = fma(aValues[row], bValues[col], sums[row][col]);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // All readers must finish before the next tile overwrites shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+        const size_t i = rowBase + ty + row * threadsY;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+            const size_t j = colBase + tx + col * threadsX;
+            if (fullTiles || (i < N && j < N)) {
+                C[i * N + j] = sums[row][col];
+            }
         }
     }
 }
 
-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,

+// Allocate once, outside the timed multiplication, and release on every exit.
+class DeviceMatrices {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceMatrices(size_t N) : elements_(N * N) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(0), "Selecting CUDA device");
+        if (elements_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                 3 * elements_ * sizeof(double)), "Allocating GPU matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceMatrices() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
         }
     }
+
+    DeviceMatrices(const DeviceMatrices&) = delete;
+    DeviceMatrices& operator=(const DeviceMatrices&) = delete;
+
+    double* A() const { return data_; }
+    double* B() const { return data_ == nullptr ? nullptr : data_ + elements_; }
+    double* C() const { return data_ == nullptr ? nullptr : data_ + 2 * elements_; }
+
+private:
+    size_t elements_;
+    double* data_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+void matrixMultiply(const DeviceMatrices& matrices, const size_t N) {
+    if (N == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const dim3 threads(threadsX, threadsY);
+    const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N + tileCols - 1) / tileCols),
+                      static_cast<unsigned>((N + tileRows - 1) / tileRows));
+    if (N % tileRows == 0 && N % tileCols == 0 && N % tileDepth == 0) {
+        matrixMultiplyKernel<true><<<blocks, threads>>>(matrices.A(), matrices.B(), matrices.C(), N);
+    } else {
+        matrixMultiplyKernel<false><<<blocks, threads>>>(matrices.A(), matrices.B(), matrices.C(), N);
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching CUDA multiplication");
+    checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "Completing CUDA multiplication");
 }

 // Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
-constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
+__host__ __device__ constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
     return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
 }
 
-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Both inputs use the same deterministic initialization as the CPU benchmark.
+__global__ void initMatrix(double* A, double* B, const size_t N) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < N * N; index += stride) {
+        const double value = getPseudoRndValue(N, index / N, index % N);
+        A[index] = value;
+        B[index] = value;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr unsigned tileRows = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileCols = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileDepth = 16;
+constexpr unsigned threadsX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned threadsY = 8;
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive columns, and each thread keeps an 8x2 output
+// tile in registers. Shared input tiles are reused for 4096 output elements.
+template <bool fullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[tileRows][tileDepth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[tileDepth][tileCols];
+
+    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned thread = ty * threadsX + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tileRows;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileCols;
+    double sums[tileRows / threadsY][tileCols / threadsX] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += tileDepth) {
+        // Coalesced global loads; edge tiles are zero padded.
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned index = thread; index < tileRows * tileDepth;
+             index += threadsX * threadsY) {
+            const unsigned row = index / tileDepth;
+            const unsigned k = index % tileDepth;
+            aTile[row][k] = (fullTiles || (rowBase + row < N && base + k < N))
+                ? A[(rowBase + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned index = thread; index < tileDepth * tileCols;
+             index += threadsX * threadsY) {
+            const unsigned k = index / tileCols;
+            const unsigned col = index % tileCols;
+            bTile[k][col] = (fullTiles || (base + k < N && colBase + col < N))
+                ? B[(base + k) * N + colBase + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        // Accumulate in increasing k order, using full-precision double FMA.
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < tileDepth; ++k) {
+            double aValues[tileRows / threadsY];
+            double bValues[tileCols / threadsX];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+                aValues[row] = aTile[ty + row * threadsY][k];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+                bValues[col] = bTile[k][tx + col * threadsX];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+                    sums[row][col] = fma(aValues[row], bValues[col], sums[row][col]);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // All readers must finish before the next tile overwrites shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+        const size_t i = rowBase + ty + row * threadsY;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+            const size_t j = colBase + tx + col * threadsX;
+            if (fullTiles || (i < N && j < N)) {
+                C[i * N + j] = sums[row][col];
+            }
         }
     }
 }
 
-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+// Allocate once, outside the timed multiplication, and release on every exit.
+class DeviceMatrices {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceMatrices(size_t N) : elements_(N * N) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(0), "Selecting CUDA device");
+        if (elements_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                 3 * elements_ * sizeof(double)), "Allocating GPU matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceMatrices() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
         }
     }
+
+    DeviceMatrices(const DeviceMatrices&) = delete;
+    DeviceMatrices& operator=(const DeviceMatrices&) = delete;
+
+    double* A() const { return data_; }
+    double* B() const { return data_ == nullptr ? nullptr : data_ + elements_; }
+    double* C() const { return data_ == nullptr ? nullptr : data_ + 2 * elements_; }
+
+private:
+    size_t elements_;
+    double* data_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+void matrixMultiply(const DeviceMatrices& matrices, const size_t N) {
+    if (N == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const dim3 threads(threadsX, threadsY);
+    const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N + tileCols - 1) / tileCols),
+                      static_cast<unsigned>((N + tileRows - 1) / tileRows));
+    if (N % tileRows == 0 && N % tileCols == 0 && N % tileDepth == 0) {
+        matrixMultiplyKernel<true><<<blocks, threads>>>(matrices.A(), matrices.B(), matrices.C(), N);
+    } else {
+        matrixMultiplyKernel<false><<<blocks, threads>>>(matrices.A(), matrices.B(), matrices.C(), N);
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching CUDA multiplication");
+    checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "Completing CUDA multiplication");
 }

 // Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
-constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
+__host__ __device__ constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
     return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
 }
 
-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Both inputs use the same deterministic initialization as the CPU benchmark.
+__global__ void initMatrix(double* A, double* B, const size_t N) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < N * N; index += stride) {
+        const double value = getPseudoRndValue(N, index / N, index % N);
+        A[index] = value;
+        B[index] = value;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr unsigned tileRows = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileCols = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileDepth = 16;
+constexpr unsigned threadsX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned threadsY = 8;
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive columns, and each thread keeps an 8x2 output
+// tile in registers. Shared input tiles are reused for 4096 output elements.
+template <bool fullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[tileRows][tileDepth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[tileDepth][tileCols];
+
+    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned thread = ty * threadsX + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tileRows;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileCols;
+    double sums[tileRows / threadsY][tileCols / threadsX] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += tileDepth) {
+        // Coalesced global loads; edge tiles are zero padded.
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned index = thread; index < tileRows * tileDepth;
+             index += threadsX * threadsY) {
+            const unsigned row = index / tileDepth;
+            const unsigned k = index % tileDepth;
+            aTile[row][k] = (fullTiles || (rowBase + row < N && base + k < N))
+                ? A[(rowBase + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned index = thread; index < tileDepth * tileCols;
+             index += threadsX * threadsY) {
+            const unsigned k = index / tileCols;
+            const unsigned col = index % tileCols;
+            bTile[k][col] = (fullTiles || (base + k < N && colBase + col < N))
+                ? B[(base + k) * N + colBase + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        // Accumulate in increasing k order, using full-precision double FMA.
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < tileDepth; ++k) {
+            double aValues[tileRows / threadsY];
+            double bValues[tileCols / threadsX];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+                aValues[row] = aTile[ty + row * threadsY][k];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+                bValues[col] = bTile[k][tx + col * threadsX];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+                    sums[row][col] = fma(aValues[row], bValues[col], sums[row][col]);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // All readers must finish before the next tile overwrites shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+        const size_t i = rowBase + ty + row * threadsY;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+            const size_t j = colBase + tx + col * threadsX;
+            if (fullTiles || (i < N && j < N)) {
+                C[i * N + j] = sums[row][col];
+            }
         }
     }
 }
 
-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+// Allocate once, outside the timed multiplication, and release on every exit.
+class DeviceMatrices {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceMatrices(size_t N) : elements_(N * N) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(0), "Selecting CUDA device");
+        if (elements_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                 3 * elements_ * sizeof(double)), "Allocating GPU matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceMatrices() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
         }
     }
+
+    DeviceMatrices(const DeviceMatrices&) = delete;
+    DeviceMatrices& operator=(const DeviceMatrices&) = delete;
+
+    double* A() const { return data_; }
+    double* B() const { return data_ == nullptr ? nullptr : data_ + elements_; }
+    double* C() const { return data_ == nullptr ? nullptr : data_ + 2 * elements_; }
+
+private:
+    size_t elements_;
+    double* data_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+void matrixMultiply(const DeviceMatrices& matrices, const size_t N) {
+    if (N == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const dim3 threads(threadsX, threadsY);
+    const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N + tileCols - 1) / tileCols),
+                      static_cast<unsigned>((N + tileRows - 1) / tileRows));
+    if (N % tileRows == 0 && N % tileCols == 0 && N % tileDepth == 0) {
+        matrixMultiplyKernel<true><<<blocks, threads>>>(matrices.A(), matrices.B(), matrices.C(), N);
+    } else {
+        matrixMultiplyKernel<false><<<blocks, threads>>>(matrices.A(), matrices.B(), matrices.C(), N);
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching CUDA multiplication");
+    checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "Completing CUDA multiplication");
 }

                        b[index] = (static_cast<int>(index*7%89)-44)/97.0;

-constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
+__host__ __device__ constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
     return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
 }
 
-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Both inputs use the same deterministic initialization as the CPU benchmark.
+__global__ void initMatrix(double* A, double* B, const size_t N) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < N * N; index += stride) {
+        const double value = getPseudoRndValue(N, index / N, index % N);
+        A[index] = value;
+        B[index] = value;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr unsigned tileRows = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileCols = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileDepth = 16;
+constexpr unsigned threadsX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned threadsY = 8;
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive columns, and each thread keeps an 8x2 output
+// tile in registers. Shared input tiles are reused for 4096 output elements.
+template <bool fullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[tileRows][tileDepth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[tileDepth][tileCols];
+
+    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned thread = ty * threadsX + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tileRows;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileCols;
+    double sums[tileRows / threadsY][tileCols / threadsX] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += tileDepth) {
+        // Coalesced global loads; edge tiles are zero padded.
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned index = thread; index < tileRows * tileDepth;
+             index += threadsX * threadsY) {
+            const unsigned row = index / tileDepth;
+            const unsigned k = index % tileDepth;
+            aTile[row][k] = (fullTiles || (rowBase + row < N && base + k < N))
+                ? A[(rowBase + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned index = thread; index < tileDepth * tileCols;
+             index += threadsX * threadsY) {
+            const unsigned k = index / tileCols;
+            const unsigned col = index % tileCols;
+            bTile[k][col] = (fullTiles || (base + k < N && colBase + col < N))
+                ? B[(base + k) * N + colBase + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        // Accumulate in increasing k order, using full-precision double FMA.
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < tileDepth; ++k) {
+            double aValues[tileRows / threadsY];
+            double bValues[tileCols / threadsX];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+                aValues[row] = aTile[ty + row * threadsY][k];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+                bValues[col] = bTile[k][tx + col * threadsX];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+                    sums[row][col] = fma(aValues[row], bValues[col], sums[row][col]);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // All readers must finish before the next tile overwrites shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+        const size_t i = rowBase + ty + row * threadsY;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+            const size_t j = colBase + tx + col * threadsX;
+            if (fullTiles || (i < N && j < N)) {
+                C[i * N + j] = sums[row][col];
+            }
         }
     }
 }
 
-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {

-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];

+// Allocate once, outside the timed multiplication, and release on every exit.
+class DeviceMatrices {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceMatrices(size_t N) : elements_(N * N) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(0), "Selecting CUDA device");
+        if (elements_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                 3 * elements_ * sizeof(double)), "Allocating GPU matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceMatrices() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
         }
     }
+
+    DeviceMatrices(const DeviceMatrices&) = delete;
+    DeviceMatrices& operator=(const DeviceMatrices&) = delete;
+
+    double* A() const { return data_; }
+    double* B() const { return data_ == nullptr ? nullptr : data_ + elements_; }
+    double* C() const { return data_ == nullptr ? nullptr : data_ + 2 * elements_; }
+
+private:
+    size_t elements_;
+    double* data_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+void matrixMultiply(const DeviceMatrices& matrices, const size_t N) {
+    if (N == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const dim3 threads(threadsX, threadsY);
+    const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N + tileCols - 1) / tileCols),
+                      static_cast<unsigned>((N + tileRows - 1) / tileRows));
+    if (N % tileRows == 0 && N % tileCols == 0 && N % tileDepth == 0) {
+        matrixMultiplyKernel<true><<<blocks, threads>>>(matrices.A(), matrices.B(), matrices.C(), N);
+    } else {
+        matrixMultiplyKernel<false><<<blocks, threads>>>(matrices.A(), matrices.B(), matrices.C(), N);
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching CUDA multiplication");
+    checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "Completing CUDA multiplication");
 }

     return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);

-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Both inputs use the same deterministic initialization as the CPU benchmark.
+__global__ void initMatrix(double* A, double* B, const size_t N) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < N * N; index += stride) {
+        const double value = getPseudoRndValue(N, index / N, index % N);
+        A[index] = value;
+        B[index] = value;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr unsigned tileRows = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileCols = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileDepth = 16;
+constexpr unsigned threadsX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned threadsY = 8;
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive columns, and each thread keeps an 8x2 output
+// tile in registers. Shared input tiles are reused for 4096 output elements.
+template <bool fullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[tileRows][tileDepth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[tileDepth][tileCols];
+
+    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned thread = ty * threadsX + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tileRows;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileCols;
+    double sums[tileRows / threadsY][tileCols / threadsX] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += tileDepth) {
+        // Coalesced global loads; edge tiles are zero padded.
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned index = thread; index < tileRows * tileDepth;
+             index += threadsX * threadsY) {
+            const unsigned row = index / tileDepth;
+            const unsigned k = index % tileDepth;
+            aTile[row][k] = (fullTiles || (rowBase + row < N && base + k < N))
+                ? A[(rowBase + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned index = thread; index < tileDepth * tileCols;
+             index += threadsX * threadsY) {
+            const unsigned k = index / tileCols;
+            const unsigned col = index % tileCols;
+            bTile[k][col] = (fullTiles || (base + k < N && colBase + col < N))
+                ? B[(base + k) * N + colBase + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        // Accumulate in increasing k order, using full-precision double FMA.
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < tileDepth; ++k) {
+            double aValues[tileRows / threadsY];
+            double bValues[tileCols / threadsX];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+                aValues[row] = aTile[ty + row * threadsY][k];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+                bValues[col] = bTile[k][tx + col * threadsX];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+                    sums[row][col] = fma(aValues[row], bValues[col], sums[row][col]);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // All readers must finish before the next tile overwrites shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned row = 0; row < tileRows / threadsY; ++row) {
+        const size_t i = rowBase + ty + row * threadsY;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned col = 0; col < tileCols / threadsX; ++col) {
+            const size_t j = colBase + tx + col * threadsX;
+            if (fullTiles || (i < N && j < N)) {
+                C[i * N + j] = sums[row][col];
+            }
         }
     }
 }
 
-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+// Allocate once, outside the timed multiplication, and release on every exit.
+class DeviceMatrices {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceMatrices(size_t N) : elements_(N * N) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(0), "Selecting CUDA device");
+        if (elements_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                 3 * elements_ * sizeof(double)), "Allocating GPU matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceMatrices() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
         }
     }
+
+    DeviceMatrices(const DeviceMatrices&) = delete;
+    DeviceMatrices& operator=(const DeviceMatrices&) = delete;
+
+    double* A() const { return data_; }
+    double* B() const { return data_ == nullptr ? nullptr : data_ + elements_; }
+    double* C() const { return data_ == nullptr ? nullptr : data_ + 2 * elements_; }
+
+private:
+    size_t elements_;
+    double* data_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+void matrixMultiply(const DeviceMatrices& matrices, const size_t N) {
+    if (N == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const dim3 threads(threadsX, threadsY);
+    const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N + tileCols - 1) / tileCols),
+                      static_cast<unsigned>((N + tileRows - 1) / tileRows));
+    if (N % tileRows == 0 && N % tileCols == 0 && N % tileDepth == 0) {
+        matrixMultiplyKernel<true><<<blocks, threads>>>(matrices.A(), matrices.B(), matrices.C(), N);
+    } else {
+        matrixMultiplyKernel<false><<<blocks, threads>>>(matrices.A(), matrices.B(), matrices.C(), N);
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching CUDA multiplication");
+    checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "Completing CUDA multiplication");
 }

                        double reference=0;
                        double scale=0;
                        for (size_t k=0; k<n; ++k) {
                            reference += a[row*n+k]*b[k*n+col];
                            scale += std::abs(a[row*n+k]*b[k*n+col]);
                        }
                        double error = std::abs(c[row*n+col]-reference)/(1+scale);
                        maxError = std::max(maxError,error);
                        if (!std::isfinite(c[row*n+col]) || error > 1e-12)
                            throw std::runtime_error("Full matrix comparison failed");
                    }
                }
                printf("Full matrix passed: N=%zu pattern=%d max scaled error=%.3e'"\\n\",n,pattern,maxError);
            }
        }
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        fprintf(stderr,\"%s\\n\",error.what());
        return 1;
    }
}
'''
